    questions_tools_test.go:39: question_answer description does not mention the disabled-switch 503: Record an answer to a stored operator question (surfaces as answered to the asker's return path). Pick one of the question's options with chosen_option_id. Typing your own words with quote is optional when you pick an option, and it is the only thing needed when you do not pick one (at least one of the two is required). Whatever you type in quote is saved exactly as written, with your choice, as the record of your decision, and the asker reads it as your answer; it is never trimmed, reworded or re-encoded (capped at 16 KiB). An answer is immutable — a changed mind is a new answer that supersedes the old one (supersedes: the id of the effective answer to replace). 409 when the question is no longer open or the supersedes target is wrong, 422 when the answer text looks like a secret (store it in the datawatch secrets store and refer to it as ${secret:name} instead). Returns 201 with the stored answer and the question's new state. Proxies POST /api/questions/{id}/answer with surface set to mcp.
    questions_tools_test.go:39: question_withdraw description does not mention the disabled-switch 503: Withdraw an open operator question (marked withdrawn; it can no longer be answered). actor is the one withdrawing (required: a withdrawal is an attribution-bearing action, unlike an answer, which defaults to the caller of record). reason is optional. 409 when the question is not open (already answered, withdrawn, or expired), 404 for an unknown id. Returns the question with its new state. Proxies POST /api/questions/{id}/withdraw.
